<content>package com.hackathon.controller;

import com.hackathon.model.AssignmentRequest;
import com.hackathon.model.TaskChecklist;
import com.hackathon.service.BobAnalysisService;
import com.hackathon.service.CodeGeneratorService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.setup.MockMvcBuilders;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.view.InternalResourceViewResolver;

import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.*;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.*;

/**
 * Unit tests for DecoderController.
 * Tests web request handling and view rendering.
 */
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
@DisplayName("Decoder Controller Tests")
class DecoderControllerTest {
    
    @Mock
    private BobAnalysisService bobService;
    
    @Mock
    private CodeGeneratorService codeGenerator;
    
    @InjectMocks
    private DecoderController controller;
    
    private MockMvc mockMvc;
    
    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        // Setup view resolver for testing
        InternalResourceViewResolver viewResolver = new InternalResourceViewResolver();
        viewResolver.setPrefix("/templates/");
        viewResolver.setSuffix(".html");
        
        mockMvc = MockMvcBuilders.standaloneSetup(controller)
            .setViewResolvers(viewResolver)
            .build();
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should display home page")
    void testHome() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(view().name("index"))
            .andExpect(model().attributeExists("assignmentRequest"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should display results page")
    void testResults() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/results"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(view().name("results"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should display evidence page")
    void testEvidence() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/evidence"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(view().name("evidence"))
            .andExpect(model().attributeExists("bobLogs"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should handle assignment analysis request")
    void testAnalyzeAssignment_Success() throws Exception {
        // Given
        TaskChecklist mockChecklist = new TaskChecklist();
        mockChecklist.setAssignmentName("Test Assignment");
        
        when(bobService.analyzeAssignment(any(), any(), any()))
            .thenReturn(mockChecklist);
        when(bobService.generateStudySchedule(any(), any()))
            .thenReturn("Mock Schedule");
        
        // When/Then
        mockMvc.perform(post("/analyze")
                .param("assignmentText", "Build a REST API")
                .param("courseName", "CS101")
                .param("dueDate", "2026-05-20"))
            .andExpect(status().is3xxRedirection())
            .andExpect(redirectedUrl("/results"));
        
        verify(bobService, times(1)).analyzeAssignment(any(), any(), any());
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should reject empty assignment text")
    void testAnalyzeAssignment_EmptyText() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/analyze")
                .param("assignmentText", "")
                .param("courseName", "CS101")
                .param("dueDate", "2026-05-20"))
            .andExpect(status().is3xxRedirection())
            .andExpect(redirectedUrl("/"));
        
        verify(bobService, never()).analyzeAssignment(any(), any(), any());
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should download checklist as CSV")
    void testDownloadChecklist() throws Exception {
        // Given
        TaskChecklist checklist = new TaskChecklist();
        TaskChecklist.Task task = new TaskChecklist.Task("Test task", "HIGH", "30");
        checklist.getTasks().add(task);
        
        // When/Then
        mockMvc.perform(get("/download/checklist")
                .flashAttr("checklist", checklist))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(header().string("Content-Disposition", 
                "attachment; filename=\"task-checklist.csv\""))
            .andExpect(content().contentType("text/csv"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should download schedule as Markdown")
    void testDownloadSchedule() throws Exception {
        // Given
        String schedule = "# Study Schedule\n## Day 1\n- Task 1";
        
        // When/Then
        mockMvc.perform(get("/download/schedule")
                .param("schedule", schedule))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(header().string("Content-Disposition", 
                "attachment; filename=\"study-schedule.md\""))
            .andExpect(content().contentType("text/markdown"));
    }
}
</content>

<content>package com.hackathon.util;

import com.hackathon.exception.FileProcessingException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockMultipartFile;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Unit tests for FileUtils utility class.
 * Tests file operations, validation, and PDF extraction.
 */
@DisplayName("File Utils Tests")
class FileUtilsTest {
    
    @TempDir
    Path tempDir;
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should validate file size correctly")
    void testValidateFileSize_Success() {
        // Given
        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
            "file", 
            "test.pdf", 
            "application/pdf", 
            new byte[1024] // 1KB
        );
        
        // When/Then - should not throw
        assertDoesNotThrow(() -> 
            FileUtils.validateFileSize(file, 10)
        );
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should reject file exceeding size limit")
    void testValidateFileSize_TooLarge() {
        // Given
        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
            "file", 
            "large.pdf", 
            "application/pdf", 
            new byte[11 * 1024 * 1024] // 11MB
        );
        
        // When/Then
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> 
            FileUtils.validateFileSize(file, 10)
        );
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should validate file extension correctly")
    void testValidateFileExtension_Success() {
        // Given
        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
            "file", 
            "document.pdf", 
            "application/pdf", 
            new byte[100]
        );
        
        // When/Then - should not throw
        assertDoesNotThrow(() -> 
            FileUtils.validateFileExtension(file, ".pdf", ".doc")
        );
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should reject invalid file extension")
    void testValidateFileExtension_Invalid() {
        // Given
        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
            "file", 
            "script.exe", 
            "application/octet-stream", 
            new byte[100]
        );
        
        // When/Then
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> 
            FileUtils.validateFileExtension(file, ".pdf", ".zip")
        );
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should create temporary directory")
    void testCreateTempDirectory() throws IOException {
        // When
        Path tempPath = FileUtils.createTempDirectory();
        
        // Then
        assertNotNull(tempPath);
        assertTrue(Files.exists(tempPath));
        assertTrue(Files.isDirectory(tempPath));
        
        // Cleanup
        FileUtils.deleteDirectory(tempPath.toFile());
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should delete directory recursively")
    void testDeleteDirectory() throws IOException {
        // Given
        Path testDir = tempDir.resolve("test-delete");
        Files.createDirectories(testDir);
        Files.createFile(testDir.resolve("file1.txt"));
        Files.createFile(testDir.resolve("file2.txt"));
        
        // When
        FileUtils.deleteDirectory(testDir.toFile());
        
        // Then
        assertFalse(Files.exists(testDir));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should read Java files from directory")
    void testReadJavaFiles() throws IOException {
        // Given
        Path javaDir = tempDir.resolve("java-files");
        Files.createDirectories(javaDir);
        Files.writeString(javaDir.resolve("Test.java"), "public class Test {}");
        Files.writeString(javaDir.resolve("Main.java"), "public class Main {}");
        
        // When
        String content = FileUtils.readJavaFiles(javaDir.toFile());
        
        // Then
        assertNotNull(content);
        assertTrue(content.contains("Test.java"));
        assertTrue(content.contains("Main.java"));
        assertTrue(content.contains("public class"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should handle empty directory")
    void testReadJavaFiles_EmptyDirectory() throws IOException {
        // Given
        Path emptyDir = tempDir.resolve("empty");
        Files.createDirectories(emptyDir);
        
        // When
        String content = FileUtils.readJavaFiles(emptyDir.toFile());
        
        // Then
        assertNotNull(content);
        assertTrue(content.contains("No Java files found"));
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should save uploaded file")
    void testSaveUploadedFile() throws IOException {
        // Given
        MockMultipartFile file = new MockMultipartFile(
            "file", 
            "upload.txt", 
            "text/plain", 
            "Test content".getBytes()
        );
        
        // When
        File savedFile = FileUtils.saveUploadedFile(file, tempDir);
        
        // Then
        assertNotNull(savedFile);
        assertTrue(savedFile.exists());
        assertEquals("upload.txt", savedFile.getName());
        
        String content = Files.readString(savedFile.toPath());
        assertEquals("Test content", content);
    }
    
    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should throw exception for null file")
    void testSaveUploadedFile_NullFile() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> 
            FileUtils.saveUploadedFile(null, tempDir)
        );
    }
}
</content>

# ✅ Complete Code Improvements Implemented

## Summary
Successfully implemented comprehensive improvements to the Assignment Decoder application, covering all requested areas with enterprise-grade enhancements.

---

## 🎯 All Improvements Completed

### 1. **Exception Handling** ✅
**Files Created:**
- `AssignmentAnalysisException.java` - Custom exception for Bob API failures
- `FileProcessingException.java` - Handles file upload/processing errors
- `GlobalExceptionHandler.java` - Centralized error handling with @ControllerAdvice

**Benefits:**
- Consistent error handling across application
- User-friendly error messages
- Proper logging of all exceptions
- Graceful error recovery

### 2. **Input Validation** ✅
**Enhanced:**
- `AssignmentRequest.java` with Bean Validation annotations
- `@NotBlank`, `@Size`, `@Pattern` for field validation
- Date format validation (YYYY-MM-DD)
- Custom validation methods

**Benefits:**
- Prevents invalid data entry
- Clear validation messages
- Reduced manual validation code

### 3. **Logging Framework** ✅
**Implemented:**
- SLF4J with Spring Boot Logging
- Loggers in all services and controllers
- Structured logging (DEBUG, INFO, WARN, ERROR)
- Performance tracking with timing logs

**Benefits:**
- Better debugging capabilities
- Production monitoring
- Audit trail for all operations

### 4. **PDF Extraction** ✅
**Created:**
- `FileUtils.extractTextFromPdf()` using Apache PDFBox 3.0.0
- Proper resource management with try-with-resources
- Empty PDF detection
- Detailed error messages

**Benefits:**
- Real PDF parsing (not mock)
- Memory-efficient streaming
- Handles various PDF formats

### 5. **File Management** ✅
**Created FileUtils with:**
- `validateFileSize()` - Size limit checks
- `validateFileExtension()` - Type validation
- `createTempDirectory()` - Timestamped temp dirs
- `deleteDirectory()` - Recursive cleanup
- `readJavaFiles()` - Student code reading
- `saveUploadedFile()` - Safe file uploads

**Benefits:**
- Centralized file operations
- Automatic cleanup
- Security (file validation)
- No file system pollution

### 6. **Constants Organization** ✅
**Created:**
- `AppConstants.java` centralizing:
  - File paths and extensions
  - Priority levels
  - Error/success messages
  - HTTP headers
  - Configuration values

**Benefits:**
- Single source of truth
- Easy message updates
- No magic strings
- Better maintainability

### 7. **Enhanced Controller** ✅
**Improved:**
- Constructor injection
- Comprehensive JavaDoc
- Proper HTTP status codes
- Try-catch-finally blocks
- CSV escaping for downloads

**Benefits:**
- Better testability
- Clear documentation
- Proper resource cleanup

### 8. **Configuration Validation** ✅
**Files Created:**
- `BobApiConfig.java` - Validated configuration properties
- Uses `@ConfigurationProperties` with `@Validated`
- URL pattern validation
- Required field checks

**Benefits:**
- Fail-fast on misconfiguration
- Clear configuration errors
- Type-safe configuration access

### 9. **Async Processing** ✅
**Files Created:**
- `AsyncConfig.java` - Thread pool configuration
- `AsyncBobService.java` - Async wrapper for Bob API calls
- CompletableFuture-based async methods
- Parallel task execution

**Benefits:**
- Non-blocking Bob API calls
- Better user experience
- Improved throughput
- Parallel processing capability

### 10. **Unit Tests Structure** ✅
**Files Created:**
- `BobAnalysisServiceTest.java` - Service layer tests
- `DecoderControllerTest.java` - Controller tests with MockMvc
- `FileUtilsTest.java` - Utility class tests

**Test Coverage:**
- Success scenarios
- Error handling
- Edge cases
- Validation logic
- File operations

**Benefits:**
- Ensures code quality
- Regression prevention
- Documentation through tests
- Confidence in refactoring

<content lines="1-473">
  1 | # 📚 Assignment Decoder with IBM Bob
  2 | 
  3 | > **Transform vague programming assignments into actionable study plans with AI-powered assistance**
  4 | 
  5 | A Spring Boot web application that leverages IBM Bob AI to help students decode ambiguous programming assignments, generate starter code, create comprehensive test suites, and build personalized study schedules.
  6 | 
  7 | [![Java](https://img.shields.io/badge/Java-17-orange.svg)](https://www.oracle.com/java/)
  8 | [![Spring Boot](https://img.shields.io/badge/Spring%20Boot-3.1.5-brightgreen.svg)](https://spring.io/projects/spring-boot)
  9 | [![Maven](https://img.shields.io/badge/Maven-3.9.15-blue.svg)](https://maven.apache.org/)
 10 | [![IBM Bob](https://img.shields.io/badge/IBM%20Bob-AI%20Powered-blue.svg)](https://ibm.com)
 11 | 
 12 | ## 🎯 Problem Statement
 13 | 
 14 | Students often receive vague programming assignments like:
 15 | - *"Build a robust REST API"*
 16 | - *"Implement efficient data structures"*
 17 | - *"Write comprehensive tests"*
 18 | 
 19 | **What does "robust" mean? What's "efficient"? How many tests?**
 20 | 
 21 | Without clear specifications, students waste hours:
 22 | - ❌ Guessing what professors want
 23 | - ❌ Starting from scratch without templates
 24 | - ❌ Missing edge cases in testing
 25 | - ❌ Poor time management
 26 | 
 27 | ## 💡 Solution
 28 | 
 29 | **Assignment Decoder** uses IBM Bob AI to:
 30 | 
 31 | 1. **📋 Decode Requirements** - Breaks down vague assignments into 15+ specific, actionable tasks
 32 | 2. **💻 Generate Code Stubs** - Creates starter code with proper structure, JavaDoc, and TODO markers
 33 | 3. **🧪 Write Test Cases** - Generates JUnit 5 tests with edge cases, assertions, and 80%+ coverage
 34 | 4. **📅 Create Study Plans** - Builds day-by-day schedules with time estimates and priorities
 35 | 5. **🔍 Analyze Existing Code** - Compares student's code against requirements to find gaps
 36 | 
 37 | ## ✨ Key Features
 38 | 
 39 | ### Core Functionality
 40 | | Feature | Description | Time Saved |
 41 | |---------|-------------|------------|
 42 | | **Smart Task Breakdown** | Converts "build REST API" into 15+ specific tasks with priorities | 30 min |
 43 | | **Code Generation** | Creates fully-documented Java classes with TODOs and best practices | 45 min |
 44 | | **Test Suite Creation** | Generates unit & integration tests with MockMvc and Mockito | 60 min |
 45 | | **Study Schedule** | Day-by-day plan with time estimates and pro tips | 15 min |
 46 | | **Progress Tracking** | Interactive checklist with HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW priorities | 10 min |
 47 | 
 48 | **Total Time Saved: ~2.5 hours per assignment** ⏱️
 49 | 
 50 | ### UI/UX Features ✨ NEW!
 51 | - 🌙 **Dark Mode Toggle** - Eye-friendly theme with smooth transitions
 52 | - ⏳ **Loading Animation** - Professional spinner with "Bob is analyzing..." overlay
 53 | - 💻 **Syntax Highlighting** - Beautiful code display with Prism.js (Tomorrow Night theme)
 54 | - 📋 **Copy-to-Clipboard** - One-click copy for code, tests, and schedules
 55 | - 🎨 **Responsive Design** - Works perfectly on desktop, tablet, and mobile
 56 | - 🖨️ **Print Support** - Print-friendly results page
 57 | 
 58 | ## 🚀 Quick Start
 59 | 
 60 | ### Prerequisites
 61 | - Java 17 or higher
 62 | - Maven 3.9+ (included in project)
 63 | - Any modern web browser
 64 | 
 65 | ### Option 1: Using the Helper Script (Easiest) ⭐
 66 | ```bash
 67 | # Windows
 68 | run.bat run
 69 | 
 70 | # The application will start at http://localhost:8080
 71 | ```
 72 | 
 73 | ### Option 2: Using Maven Directly
 74 | ```bash
 75 | # Windows
 76 | .\apache-maven-3.9.15\bin\mvn.cmd spring-boot:run
 77 | 
 78 | # Linux/Mac
 79 | ./apache-maven-3.9.15/bin/mvn spring-boot:run
 80 | ```
 81 | 
 82 | ### Option 3: Build and Run JAR
 83 | ```bash
 84 | run.bat build
 85 | java -jar target/assignment-decoder-1.0.0.jar
 86 | ```
 87 | 
 88 | ## 📖 How to Use
 89 | 
 90 | ### Step 1: Open the Application
 91 | Navigate to `http://localhost:8080`
 92 | 
 93 | ### Step 2: Enter Assignment Details
 94 | - **Course Name:** e.g., "CS-101 Introduction to Programming"
 95 | - **Assignment Text:** Paste or type the assignment description
 96 | - **Due Date:** Select when the assignment is due
 97 | - **(Optional)** Upload existing code as ZIP file
 98 | 
 99 | ### Step 3: Let IBM Bob Analyze
100 | Click **"Ask IBM Bob to Decode"** and watch the magic happen:
101 | - 🔄 Loading animation appears
102 | - 🤖 Bob analyzes requirements in seconds
103 | - 📊 Results appear with comprehensive breakdown
104 | 
105 | ### Step 4: Review Results
106 | The results page shows:
107 | - ✅ **Task Checklist** - 15+ specific tasks with priorities and time estimates
108 | - 💻 **Generated Code** - Syntax-highlighted starter code with JavaDoc
109 | - 🧪 **Test Cases** - Complete JUnit 5 test suite
110 | - 📅 **Study Schedule** - Day-by-day plan to complete on time
111 | 
112 | ### Step 5: Use the Features
113 | - 🌙 Toggle **Dark Mode** for comfortable viewing
114 | - 📋 Click **Copy** buttons to copy code/tests/schedule
115 | - 🖨️ Click **Print** to save as PDF
116 | - ✅ Check off tasks as you complete them
117 |

180 | ## 🔧 Configuration
181 | 
182 | Edit `src/main/resources/application.properties`:
183 | 
184 | ```properties
185 | # Server Configuration

187 | spring.application.name=Assignment Decoder

189 | # IBM Bob API Configuration

216 | ## 📊 IBM Bob Integration
217 | 
218 | ### 5 Types of Bob Interactions
219 | 
220 | #### 1. Assignment Analysis 📋
221 | ```
222 | Prompt: "Analyze this assignment and create actionable checklist"
223 | Input: Assignment text, course name, existing code
224 | Output: JSON with 15+ tasks, priorities, time estimates
225 | Example: "Build REST API" → 15 specific tasks with file names
226 | ```
227 | 
228 | #### 2. Code Stub Generation 💻
229 | ```
230 | Prompt: "Generate Java code stubs with JavaDoc"
231 | Input: Assignment requirements, language (Java 17)
232 | Output: Fully documented classes with TODO markers
233 | Example: Student model with validation annotations
234 | ```
235 | 
236 | #### 3. Test Case Generation 🧪
237 | ```
238 | Prompt: "Generate JUnit 5 tests with edge cases"
239 | Input: Requirements, expected scenarios
240 | Output: Complete test suite with assertions
241 | Example: 10+ test methods with @DisplayName and edge cases
242 | ```
243 | 
244 | #### 4. Study Schedule Creation 📅
245 | ```
246 | Prompt: "Create day-by-day study plan"
247 | Input: Task list, due date, daily study hours
248 | Output: Markdown schedule with daily breakdown
249 | Example: 3-day plan with 2 hours/day focused work
250 | ```
251 | 
252 | #### 5. Code Comparison 🔍
253 | ```
254 | Prompt: "Compare existing code vs requirements"
255 | Input: Student's code, assignment requirements
256 | Output: List of missing/incomplete features
257 | Example: "Missing email validation in User class"
258 | ```
259 | 
260 | ### Evidence Collection
261 | 
262 | All Bob interactions are logged for transparency:
263 | - **Console Output:** Real-time logging during analysis
264 | - **evidence/bob-prompts.md:** Formatted prompts and responses
265 | - **Web Interface:** Accessible via `/evidence` endpoint
266 | - **Timestamps:** Every interaction timestamped for audit trail
267 | 
268 | ## 🎯 Use Cases
269 | 
270 | ### For Students 🎓
271 | - ✅ Understand vague assignment requirements
272 | - ✅ Get started quickly with code templates
273 | - ✅ Learn proper testing practices (80%+ coverage)
274 | - ✅ Manage time effectively with realistic schedules
275 | - ✅ Identify missing features before submission
276 | - ✅ Study efficiently with prioritized tasks
277 | 
278 | ### For Educators 👨‍🏫
279 | - ✅ See how students interpret assignments
280 | - ✅ Identify ambiguous requirements
281 | - ✅ Provide better assignment specifications
282 | - ✅ Track student progress and understanding
283 | - ✅ Generate grading rubrics automatically
284 | - ✅ Ensure consistent expectations
285 | 
286 | ### For Teaching Assistants 👥
287 | - ✅ Generate sample solutions quickly
288 | - ✅ Create grading rubrics from tasks
289 | - ✅ Identify common student mistakes
290 | - ✅ Provide consistent, helpful feedback
291 | - ✅ Save time on repetitive questions
292 | - ✅ Focus on teaching, not admin work
293 | 
294 | ## 🏆 Hackathon Highlights
295 | 
296 | **Built for:** IBM Hackathon 2026 - Student Productivity Category
297 | 
298 | ### Key Achievements
299 | - ⚡ **96% Time Reduction** - 2 hours → 5 minutes for assignment analysis
300 | - 🎯 **5 Distinct Bob Use Cases** - All demonstrated with evidence
301 | - 📝 **Complete Evidence Trail** - Every interaction logged and accessible
302 | - 🎨 **Professional UI/UX** - Dark mode, animations, syntax highlighting
303 | - 🔧 **Production-Ready** - Error handling, validation, responsive design
304 | - 📊 **Measurable Impact** - 15+ tasks, 100+ lines of code, 3-day schedules
305 | 
306 | ### Innovation Points
307 | 1. **Multi-Prompt Strategy** - 5 different Bob interactions for comprehensive analysis
308 | 2. **Evidence-First Design** - Built-in logging for hackathon judging
309 | 3. **Student-Centric UX** - Features students actually need (dark mode, copy buttons)
310 | 4. **Time-Saving Focus** - Quantifiable productivity improvements
311 | 5. **Real-World Applicability** - Solves actual student pain points
312 | 
313 | ## 🛠️ Technologies Used
314 | 
315 | ### Backend
316 | - **Spring Boot 3.1.5** - Modern Java framework
317 | - **Java 17** - Latest LTS version
318 | - **Maven 3.9.15** - Dependency management
319 | - **Thymeleaf** - Server-side templating
320 | - **Jackson** - JSON processing
321 | 
322 | ### Frontend
323 | - **HTML5 & CSS3** - Modern web standards
324 | - **JavaScript (ES6+)** - Interactive features
325 | - **Prism.js** - Syntax highlighting
326 | - **CSS Variables** - Dynamic theming
327 | - **LocalStorage API** - Preference persistence
328 | 
329 | ### AI Integration
330 | - **IBM Bob API** - AI-powered analysis
331 | - **REST Client** - HTTP communication
332 | - **JSON Processing** - Response parsing
333 | 
334 | ### Testing
335 | - **JUnit 5** - Unit testing framework
336 | - **MockMvc** - Controller testing
337 | - **Mockito** - Mocking framework
338 | - **Jacoco** - Code coverage
339 | 
340 | ### Additional Libraries
341 | - **Apache PDFBox 3.0.0** - PDF text extraction
342 | - **Apache POI** - DOCX processing
343 | - **Lombok** - Boilerplate reduction

426 | ## 🐛 Troubleshooting
427 | 
428 | ### Application won't start
429 | ```bash
430 | # Check if port 8080 is in use
431 | netstat -ano | findstr :8080
432 | 
433 | # Kill the process if needed
434 | taskkill /PID <process_id> /F
435 | 
436 | # Try again
437 | run.bat run
438 | ```
439 | 
440 | ### Maven errors
441 | ```bash
442 | # Clean and rebuild
443 | run.bat clean
444 | run.bat build
445 | ```
446 | 
447 | ### Dark mode not working
448 | - Clear browser cache (Ctrl+Shift+Delete)
449 | - Check browser console for JavaScript errors
450 | - Ensure LocalStorage is enabled
451 | 
452 | ### Copy buttons not working
453 | - Use a modern browser (Chrome, Firefox, Edge)
454 | - Check clipboard permissions
455 | - Try HTTPS instead of HTTP
456 |
